```python
from main import Graph, Node, Edge, NODE, EDGE, ATTR


class TestDotDSL(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_graph_with_no_data(self):
        graph = Graph()
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_with_attributes(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (ATTR, "foo", "bar"),
            (ATTR, "bat", "baz")
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"foo": "bar", "bat": "baz"})

    def test_graph_with_nodes(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "green"}),
            (NODE, "b", {"label": "Beta!"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [
            Node("a", {"color": "green"}),
            Node("b", {"label": "Beta!"})
        ])

    def test_graph_with_edges(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "blue"}),
            (EDGE, "c", "d", {"style": "dotted"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [
            Edge("a", "b", {"color": "blue"}),
            Edge("c", "d", {"style": "dotted"})
        ])

    def test_graph_with_mixed_elements(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (ATTR, "foo", "bar"),
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "green"}),
            (NODE, "b", {"label": "Beta!"}),
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "blue"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"foo": "bar"})
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [
            Node("a", {"color": "green"}),
            Node("b", {"label": "Beta!"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [
            Edge("a", "b", {"color": "blue"})
        ])

    def test_graph_data_not_list(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph("not a list")
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph data malformed")

    def test_graph_data_none(self):
        graph = Graph(None)
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_item_not_tuple(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph(["not a tuple"])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_graph_item_empty_tuple(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([()])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_graph_item_too_short(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([(ATTR, "only_key")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_graph_item_too_long_for_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(ATTR, "key", "value", "extra")])

import unittest
from main import Graph, Node, Edge, NODE, EDGE, ATTR


class DotDSLTest(unittest.TestCase):

    # 1) successful construction/usage
    def test_empty_graph(self):
        g = Graph([])
        self.assertEqual(g.data, [])

    def test_graph_with_single_node(self):
        g = Graph([(NODE, "a", {"color": "red"})])
        self.assertEqual(len(g.data), 1)
        self.assertEqual(g.data[0], (NODE, "a", {"color": "red"}))

    def test_graph_with_single_edge(self):
        g = Graph([(EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "green"})])
        self.assertEqual(len(g.data), 1)
        self.assertEqual(g.data[0], (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "green"}))

    def test_graph_with_single_attr(self):
        g = Graph([(ATTR, "bgcolor", "yellow")])
        self.assertEqual(len(g.data), 1)
        self.assertEqual(g.data[0], (ATTR, "bgcolor", "yellow"))

    def test_graph_with_mixed_items(self):
        data = [
            (ATTR, "bgcolor", "yellow"),
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "red"}),
            (NODE, "b", {"color": "blue"}),
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "green"})
        ]
        g = Graph(data)
        self.assertEqual(g.data, data)

    # 2) invalid input type
    def test_graph_data_not_list_raises_type_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph data malformed"):
            Graph("not a list")

    def test_graph_data_none_raises_type_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph data malformed"):
            Graph(None)

    # 3) shape/arity errors (missing/extra elements)
    def test_graph_item_empty_tuple_raises_type_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph item incomplete"):
            Graph([()])

    def test_graph_item_single_element_tuple_raises_type_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph item incomplete"):
            Graph([(NODE,)])

    def test_node_tuple_missing_attrs_raises_type_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph item incomplete"):
            Graph([(NODE, "a")])

    def test_edge_tuple_missing_attrs_raises_type_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph item incomplete"):
            Graph([(EDGE, "a", "b")])

    def test_attr_tuple_missing_value_raises_type_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph item incomplete"):
            Graph([(ATTR, "bgcolor")])

    # 4) value/type validation errors (wrong types for fields)
    def test_node_with_non_string_name_raises_value_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Node is malformed"):
            Graph([(NODE, 123, {})])

    def test_node_with_non_dict_attrs_raises_value_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Node is malformed"):
            Graph([(NODE, "a", "not a dict")])

    def test_edge_with_non_string_src_raises_value_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Edge is malformed"):
            Graph([(EDGE, 123, "b", {})])

    def test_edge_with_non_string_dst_raises_value_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Edge is malformed"):
            Graph([(EDGE, "a", 123, {})])

    def test_edge_with_non_dict_attrs_raises_value_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Edge is malformed"):
            Graph([(EDGE, "a", "b", "not a dict")])

    def test_attr_with_non_string_key_raises_value_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Attribute is malformed"):
            Graph([(ATTR, 123, "value")])

    # 5) unknown/unsupported case
    def test_unknown_item_type_raises_value_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Unknown item"):
            Graph([(999, "a", "b", {})])

import unittest
from main import Graph, Node, Edge, NODE, EDGE, ATTR


class DotDSLTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_empty_graph(self):
        graph = Graph()
        self.assertEqual(graph.data, [])

    def test_graph_with_attributes(self):
        graph = Graph([(ATTR, "color", "red")])
        self.assertEqual(graph.data, [("attr", "color", "red")])

    def test_graph_with_node(self):
        graph = Graph([(NODE, "a", {"color": "red"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.data, [("node", "a", {"color": "red"})])

    def test_graph_with_edge(self):
        graph = Graph([(EDGE, "a", "b", {"weight": 1})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.data, [("edge", "a", "b", {"weight": 1})])

    def test_graph_with_multiple_items(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (ATTR, "bgcolor", "yellow"),
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "red"}),
            (NODE, "b", {"color": "blue"}),
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "green"}),
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.data, [
            ("attr", "bgcolor", "yellow"),
            ("node", "a", {"color": "red"}),
            ("node", "b", {"color": "blue"}),
            ("edge", "a", "b", {"color": "green"}),
        ])

    def test_invalid_graph_data_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as ctx:
            Graph("not a list")
        self.assertEqual(str(ctx.exception), "Graph data malformed")

    def test_empty_tuple_in_graph(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as ctx:
            Graph([()])
        self.assertEqual(str(ctx.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_incomplete_attribute(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as ctx:
            Graph([(ATTR, "color")])
        self.assertEqual(str(ctx.exception), "Attribute is malformed")

    def test_incomplete_node(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as ctx:
            Graph([(NODE, "a")])
        self.assertEqual(str(ctx.exception), "Node is malformed")

    def test_incomplete_edge(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as ctx:
            Graph([(EDGE, "a", "b")])
        self.assertEqual(str(ctx.exception), "Edge is malformed")

    def test_node_with_invalid_attrs_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as ctx:
            Graph([(NODE, "a", "not a dict")])
        self.assertEqual(str(ctx.exception), "Node is malformed")

    def test_edge_with_invalid_attrs_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as ctx:
            Graph([(EDGE, "a", "b", "not a dict")])
        self.assertEqual(str(ctx.exception), "Edge is malformed")

    def test_attribute_with_invalid_value_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as ctx:
            Graph([(ATTR, "color", 123)])
        self.assertEqual(str(ctx.exception), "Attribute is malformed")

    def test_unknown_item_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as ctx:
            Graph([(99, "unknown", "item")])
        self.assertEqual(str(ctx.exception), "Unknown item")

    def test_node_with_empty_name(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as ctx:
            Graph([(NODE, "", {"color": "red"})])
        self.assertEqual(str(ctx.exception), "Node is malformed")

    def test_edge_with_empty_nodes(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as ctx:
            Graph([(EDGE, "", "", {"weight": 1})])
        self.assertEqual(str(ctx.exception), "Edge is malformed")

    def test_attribute_with_empty_key(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as ctx:
            Graph([(ATTR, "", "red")])
        self.assertEqual(str(ctx.exception), "Attribute is malformed")

    def test_graph_with_mixed_valid_and_invalid_items(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            Graph([
                (ATTR, "color", "red"),
                (NODE, "a", "not a dict"),
                (EDGE, "a", "b", {"weight": 1}),
            ])

import unittest
from main import Graph, NODE, EDGE, ATTR, Node, Edge

class GraphTests(unittest.TestCase):
    # Successful construction tests
    def test_empty_graph(self):
        graph = Graph([])
        self.assertEqual(len(graph.nodes), 0)
        self.assertEqual(len(graph.edges), 0)
        self.assertEqual(len(graph.attrs), 0)

    def test_graph_with_single_attribute(self):
        graph = Graph([(ATTR, "color", "red")])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"color": "red"})

    def test_graph_with_single_node(self):
        graph = Graph([(NODE, "A", {"color": "blue"})])
        self.assertEqual(len(graph.nodes), 1)
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes[0], Node("A", {"color": "blue"}))

    def test_graph_with_single_edge(self):
        graph = Graph([(EDGE, "A", "B", {"color": "green"})])
        self.assertEqual(len(graph.edges), 1)
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges[0], Edge("A", "B", {"color": "green"}))

    def test_graph_with_multiple_items(self):
        data = [
            (ATTR, "bgcolor", "yellow"),
            (NODE, "A", {"color": "red"}),
            (NODE, "B", {"color": "blue"}),
            (EDGE, "A", "B", {"color": "green"})
        ]
        graph = Graph(data)
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"bgcolor": "yellow"})
        self.assertEqual(len(graph.nodes), 2)
        self.assertEqual(len(graph.edges), 1)

    # Invalid input type tests
    def test_graph_with_non_list_input(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph("not a list")
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph data malformed")

    def test_graph_with_non_tuple_item(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([123])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    # Shape/arity errors
    def test_attribute_with_insufficient_elements(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([(ATTR, "color")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_attribute_with_excessive_elements(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([(ATTR, "color", "red", "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_node_with_insufficient_elements(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([(NODE, "A")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_node_with_excessive_elements(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([(NODE, "A", {"color": "red"}, "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_edge_with_insufficient_elements(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, "A", "B")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_edge_with_excessive_elements(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, "A", "B", {"color": "green"}, "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    # Value/type validation errors
    def test_attribute_with_non_string_name(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(ATTR, 123, "red")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Attribute is malformed")

    def test_node_with_non_string_name(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(NODE, 123, {"color": "red"})])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Node is malformed")

    def test_node_with_non_dict_attributes(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(NODE, "A", "not a dict")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Node is malformed")

    def test_edge_with_non_string_source(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, 123, "B", {"color": "green"})])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Edge is malformed")

    def test_edge_with_non_string_destination(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, "A", 123, {"color": "green"})])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Edge is malformed")

    def test_edge_with_non_dict_attributes(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, "A", "B", "not a dict")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Edge is malformed")

    # Unknown/unsupported case
    def test_unknown_item_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(99, "unknown", "item")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Unknown item")

This takes a `list` of one \nor more tuples that describe:\n\n+ attributes\n+ `Nodes`\n+ `Edges`\n\nThe implementations of a `Node` and an `Edge` are provided in `dot_dsl.py`.\n\nFor more details on the DSL's expected design and the expected error types and messages, take a look at the test cases in `dot_dsl_test.py` \n\n\n## Exception messages\n\nSometimes it is necessary to [raise an exception](https://docs.python.org/3/tutorial/errors.html#raising-exceptions). This takes a `list` of one \nor more tuples that describe:\n\n+ attributes\n+ `Nodes`\n+ `Edges`\n\nThe implementations of a `Node` and an `Edge` are provided in `dot_dsl.py`.\n\nFor more details on the DSL's expected design and the expected error types and messages, take a look at the test cases in `dot_dsl_test.py` \n\n\n## Exception messages\n\nSometimes it is necessary to [raise an exception](https://docs.python.org/3/tutorial/errors.html#raising-exceptions). When you do this, you should always include a **meaningful error message** to indicate what the source of the error is. This makes your code more readable and helps significantly with debugging. For situations where you know that the error source will be a certain type, you can choose to raise one of the [built in error types](https://docs.python.org/3/library/exceptions.html#base-classes), but should still include a meaningful message.\n\nThis particular exercise requires that you use the [raise statement](https://docs.python.org/3/reference/simple_stmts.html#the-raise-statement) to \"throw\" a `TypeError` for when a `Graph` is malformed, and a `ValueError` when an `Edge`, `Node`, or `attribute` is malformed. The tests will only pass if you both `raise` the `exception` and include a message with it.\n\nTo raise an error with a message, write the message as an argument to the `exception` type:\n\n```python\n# Graph is malformed\nraise TypeError(\"Graph data malformed\")\n\n# Graph item is incomplete (empty or incomplete tuple)\nraise TypeError(\"Graph item incomplete\")\n\n# Attribute has incorrect values\nraise ValueError(\"Attribute is malformed\")\n\n# Node has incorrect values\nraise ValueError(\"Node is malformed\")\n\n# Edge has incorrect values\nraise ValueError(\"Edge is malformed\")\n\n# Unknown item type\nraise ValueError(\"Unknown item\")\n```\n\n\nTask: Given a list of unittest methods, score each one's correctness vs the spec (0-100).\nReturn ONLY JSON mapping id->score, e.g., {\"m1\": 80, \"m2\": 55}."

This takes a `list` of one \nor more tuples that describe:\n\n+ attributes\n+ `Nodes`\n+ `Edges`\n\nThe implementations of a `Node` and an `Edge` are provided in `dot_dsl.py`.\n\nFor more details on the DSL's expected design and the expected error types and messages, take a look at the test cases in `dot_dsl_test.py` \n\n\n## Exception messages\n\nSometimes it is necessary to [raise an exception](https://docs.python.org/3/tutorial/errors.html#raising-exceptions). When you do this, you should always include a **meaningful error message** to indicate what the source of the error is. This makes your code more readable and helps significantly with debugging. For situations where you know that the error source will be a certain type, you can choose to raise one of the [built in error types](https://docs.python.org/3/library/exceptions.html#base-classes), but should still include a meaningful message.\n\nThis particular exercise requires that you use the [raise statement](https://docs.python.org/3/reference/simple_stmts.html#the-raise-statement) to \"throw\" a `TypeError` for when a `Graph` is malformed, and a `ValueError` when an `Edge`, `Node`, or `attribute` is malformed. The tests will only pass if you both `raise` the `exception` and include a message with it.\n\nTo raise an error with a message, write the message as an argument to the `exception` type:\n\n```python\n# Graph is malformed\nraise TypeError(\"Graph data malformed\")\n\n# Graph item is incomplete (empty or incomplete tuple)\nraise TypeError(\"Graph item incomplete\")\n\n# Attribute has incorrect values\nraise ValueError(\"Attribute is malformed\")\n\n# Node has incorrect values\nraise ValueError(\"Node is malformed\")\n\n# Edge has incorrect values\nraise ValueError(\"Edge is malformed\")\n\n# Unknown item type\nraise ValueError(\"Unknown item\")\n```\n\n\nTask: Given a list of unittest methods, score each one's correctness vs the spec (0-100).\nReturn ONLY JSON mapping id->score, e.g., {\"m1\": 80, \"m2\": 55}."

import unittest
from main import *

class GeneratedTests(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_graph_with_no_data(self):
        graph = Graph()
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_attributes(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (ATTR, "foo", "bar"),
            (ATTR, "bat", "baz")
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"foo": "bar", "bat": "baz"})


    def test_graph_with_nodes(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "green"}),
            (NODE, "b", {"label": "Beta!"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [
            Node("a", {"color": "green"}),
            Node("b", {"label": "Beta!"})
        ])


    def test_graph_with_edges(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "blue"}),
            (EDGE, "c", "d", {"style": "dotted"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [
            Edge("a", "b", {"color": "blue"}),
            Edge("c", "d", {"style": "dotted"})
        ])


    def test_graph_with_mixed_elements(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (ATTR, "foo", "bar"),
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "green"}),
            (NODE, "b", {"label": "Beta!"}),
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "blue"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"foo": "bar"})
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [
            Node("a", {"color": "green"}),
            Node("b", {"label": "Beta!"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [
            Edge("a", "b", {"color": "blue"})
        ])

        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph("not a list")

```python
# main.py
NODE, EDGE, ATTR = range(3)


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name: str, attrs: dict):
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src: str, dst: str, attrs: dict):
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return (self.src == other.src and
                self.dst == other.dst and
                self.attrs == other.attrs)


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data: list | None = None):
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}
        
        if data is None:
            return
            
        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")
            
        for item in data:
            if not isinstance(item, tuple) or len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            item_type = item[0]
            
            if item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if not isinstance(item[1], str) or not isinstance(item[2], str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[item[1]] = item[2]
                
            elif item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if not isinstance(item[1], str) or not isinstance(item[2], dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(item[1], item[2]))
                
            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) != 4:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if not isinstance(item[1], str) or not isinstance(item[2], str) or not isinstance(item[3], dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(item[1], item[2], item[3]))
                
            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")

```

```python
# main.py
NODE, EDGE, ATTR = range(3)


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name: str, attrs: dict):
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src: str, dst: str, attrs: dict):
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return (self.src == other.src and
                self.dst == other.dst and
                self.attrs == other.attrs)


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data: list | None = None):
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}

        if data is None:
            return

        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")

        for item in data:
            if not isinstance(item, tuple) or len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")

            item_type = item[0]

            if item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                key, value = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value

            elif item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                name, attrs = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(name, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))

            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) != 4:
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                src, dst, attrs = item[1], item[2], item[3]
                if not isinstance(src, str) or not isinstance(dst, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))

            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")
```

```python
# main.py
NODE, EDGE, ATTR = range(3)


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name: str, attrs: dict):
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src: str, dst: str, attrs: dict):
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return (self.src == other.src and
                self.dst == other.dst and
                self.attrs == other.attrs)


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data: list | None = None):
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}
        
        if data is None:
            return
            
        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")
            
        for item in data:
            if not isinstance(item, tuple):
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            if len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            item_type = item[0]
            
            if item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) < 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 3:
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                key, value = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value
                
            elif item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) < 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 3:
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                name, attrs = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(name, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))
                
            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) < 4:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 4:
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                src, dst, attrs = item[1], item[2], item[3]
                if not isinstance(src, str) or not isinstance(dst, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))
                
            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")
```

```python
# main.py
NODE, EDGE, ATTR = range(3)


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name: str, attrs: dict):
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src: str, dst: str, attrs: dict):
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return (self.src == other.src and
                self.dst == other.dst and
                self.attrs == other.attrs)


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data: list | None = None):
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}
        
        if data is None:
            return
            
        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")
            
        for item in data:
            if not isinstance(item, tuple):
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            if len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            item_type = item[0]
            
            if item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                key, value = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value
                
            elif item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                name, attrs = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(name, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))
                
            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) != 4:
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                src, dst, attrs = item[1], item[2], item[3]
                if not isinstance(src, str) or not isinstance(dst, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))
                
            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")
```

This takes a `list` of one \nor more tuples that describe:\n\n+ attributes\n+ `Nodes`\n+ `Edges`\n\nThe implementations of a `Node` and an `Edge` are provided in `dot_dsl.py`.\n\nFor more details on the DSL's expected design and the expected error types and messages, take a look at the test cases in `dot_dsl_test.py` \n\n\n## Exception messages\n\nSometimes it is necessary to [raise an exception](https://docs.python.org/3/tutorial/errors.html#raising-exceptions). When you do this, you should always include a **meaningful error message** to indicate what the source of the error is. This makes your code more readable and helps significantly with debugging. attrs\n\n    def __eq__(self, other):\n        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs\n\n\nclass Edge:\n    def __init__(self, src: str, dst: str, attrs: dict):\n        self.src None:\n            return\n            \n        if not isinstance(data, list):\n            raise TypeError(\"Graph data malformed\")\n            \n        for item in data:\n            if not import unittest
from main import Graph, Node, Edge, NODE, EDGE, ATTR


class DotDslTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_empty_graph(self):
        graph = Graph()
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_with_one_node(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (NODE, "a", {})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_with_one_node_with_attrs(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "green"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {"color": "green"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_with_one_edge(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_with_one_edge_with_attrs(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "blue"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {"color": "blue"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_with_one_attribute(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (ATTR, "foo", "1")
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"foo": "1"})

    def test_graph_with_multiple_items(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (ATTR, "foo", "1"),
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "green"}),
            (NODE, "b", {}),
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "blue"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {"color": "green"}), Node("b", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {"color": "blue"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"foo": "1"})

    def test_malformed_graph_data_not_list(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as cm:
            Graph("not a list")
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Graph data malformed")

    def test_malformed_graph_item_not_tuple(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as cm:
            Graph(["not a tuple"])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

            Graph([()])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_malformed_attribute_too_many_values(self):

import unittest
from main import Graph, Node, Edge, NODE, EDGE, ATTR


class DotDSLTest(unittest.TestCase):

    # 1) successful construction/usage
    def test_empty_graph(self):
        g = Graph()
        self.assertEqual(g.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(g.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(g.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_with_single_attr(self):
        g = Graph([(ATTR, "bgcolor", "yellow")])
        self.assertEqual(g.attrs, {"bgcolor": "yellow"})

    def test_graph_with_node(self):
        g = Graph([(NODE, "a", {"color": "red"})])
        self.assertEqual(len(g.nodes), 1)
        self.assertEqual(g.nodes[0], Node("a", {"color": "red"}))

    def test_graph_with_edge(self):
        g = Graph([(EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "green"})])
        self.assertEqual(len(g.edges), 1)
        self.assertEqual(g.edges[0], Edge("a", "b", {"color": "green"}))

    def test_full_example(self):
        data = [
            (ATTR, "bgcolor", "yellow"),
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "red"}),
            (NODE, "b", {"color": "blue"}),
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "green"})
        ]
        g = Graph(data)
        self.assertEqual(g.attrs, {"bgcolor": "yellow"})
        self.assertEqual(len(g.nodes), 2)
        self.assertEqual(len(g.edges), 1)
        self.assertEqual(g.edges[0], Edge("a", "b", {"color": "green"}))

    # 2) invalid input type
    def test_graph_data_not_list(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph data malformed"):
            Graph("not a list")

    # 3) shape/arity errors
    def test_graph_item_empty_tuple(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph item incomplete"):
            Graph([()])

    def test_attr_item_missing_elements(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph item incomplete"):
            Graph([(ATTR, "key")])

    def test_node_item_missing_elements(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph item incomplete"):
            Graph([(NODE, "name")])

    def test_edge_item_missing_elements(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph item incomplete"):
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", "dst")])

    # 4) value/type validation errors
    def test_attr_item_extra_element(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Attribute is malformed"):
            Graph([(ATTR, "key", "value", "extra")])

    def test_node_item_extra_element(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Node is malformed"):
            Graph([(NODE, "name", {}, "extra")])

    def test_edge_item_extra_element(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Edge is malformed"):
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", "dst", {}, "extra")])

    def test_attr_non_string_key(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Attribute is malformed"):
            Graph([(ATTR, 123, "value")])

    def test_attr_non_string_value(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Attribute is malformed"):
            Graph([(ATTR, "key", 123)])

    def test_node_non_string_name(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Node is malformed"):
            Graph([(NODE, 123, {})])

    def test_node_non_dict_attrs(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Node is malformed"):
            Graph([(NODE, "name", "not a dict")])

    def test_edge_non_string_src(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Edge is malformed"):
            Graph([(EDGE, 123, "dst", {})])

    def test_edge_non_string_dst(self):

            Graph([(EDGE, "src", 123, {})])

    def test_edge_non_dict_attrs(self):

import unittest
from main import Graph, Node, Edge, NODE, EDGE, ATTR

class DotDslTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_empty_graph(self):
        graph = Graph()
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_with_attributes(self):
        data = [(ATTR, "bgcolor", "yellow")]
        graph = Graph(data)
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"bgcolor": "yellow"})

    def test_graph_with_nodes(self):
        data = [(NODE, "a", {"color": "red"}), (NODE, "b", {"color": "blue"})]
        graph = Graph(data)
        self.assertEqual(len(graph.nodes), 2)
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes[0].name, "a")
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes[0].attrs, {"color": "red"})
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes[1].name, "b")
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes[1].attrs, {"color": "blue"})

    def test_graph_with_edges(self):
        data = [(EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "green"})]

        self.assertEqual(len(graph.edges), 1)
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges[0].src, "a")
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges[0].dst, "b")
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges[0].attrs, {"color": "green"})

        data = [
            (ATTR, "bgcolor", "yellow"),

        ]

This takes a `list` of one \nor more tuples that describe:\n\n+ attributes\n+ `Nodes`\n+ `Edges`\n\nThe implementations of a `Node` and an `Edge` are provided in `dot_dsl.py`.\n\nFor more details on the DSL's expected design and the expected error types and messages, take a look at the test cases in `dot_dsl_test.py` \n\n\n## Exception messages\n\nSometimes it is necessary to [raise an exception](https://docs.python.org/3/tutorial/errors.html#raising-exceptions). When you do this, you should always include a **meaningful error message** to indicate what the source of the error is. This makes your code more readable and helps significantly with debugging. For situations where you know that the error source will be a certain type, you can choose to raise one of the [built in error types](https://docs.python.org/3/library/exceptions.html#base-classes), but should still include a meaningful message.\n\nThis particular exercise requires that you use the [raise statement](https://docs.python.org/3/reference/simple_stmts.html#the-raise-statement) to \"throw\" a `TypeError` for when a `Graph` is malformed, and a `ValueError` when an `Edge`, `Node`, or `attribute` is malformed. The tests will only pass if you both `raise` the `exception` and include a message with it.\n\nTo raise an error with a message, write the message as an argument to the `exception` type:\n\n```python\n# Graph is malformed\nraise TypeError(\"Graph data malformed\")\n\n# Graph item is incomplete (empty or incomplete tuple)\nraise TypeError(\"Graph item incomplete\")\n\n# Attribute has incorrect values\nraise ValueError(\"Attribute is malformed\")\n\n# Node has incorrect values\nraise ValueError(\"Node is malformed\")\n\n# Edge has incorrect values\nraise ValueError(\"Edge is malformed\")\n\n# Unknown item type\nraise ValueError(\"Unknown item\")\n```\n\n\nTask: Given a list of unittest methods, score each one's correctness vs the spec (0-100).\nReturn ONLY JSON mapping id->score, e.g., {\"m1\": 80, \"m2\": 55}."

This takes a `list` of one \nor more tuples that describe:\n\n+ attributes\n+ `Nodes`\n+ `Edges`\n\nThe implementations of a `Node` and an `Edge` are provided in `dot_dsl.py`.\n\nFor more details on the DSL's expected design and the expected error types and messages, take a look at the test cases in `dot_dsl_test.py` \n\n\n## Exception messages\n\nSometimes it is necessary to [raise an exception](https://docs.python.org/3/tutorial/errors.html#raising-exceptions). When you do this, you should always include a **meaningful error message** to indicate what the source of the error is. This makes your code more readable and helps significantly with debugging. For situations where you know that the error source will be a certain type, you can choose to raise one of the [built in error types](https://docs.python.org/3/library/exceptions.html#base-classes), but should still include a meaningful message.\n\nThis particular exercise requires that you use the [raise statement](https://docs.python.org/3/reference/simple_stmts.html#the-raise-statement) to \"throw\" a `TypeError` for when a `Graph` is malformed, and a `ValueError` when an `Edge`, `Node`, or `attribute` is malformed. import unittest
from main import *

class GeneratedTests(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_empty_graph(self):
        graph = Graph()
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_node(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (NODE, "a", {})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_node_with_attrs(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "green"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {"color": "green"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_edge(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_edge_with_attrs(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "blue"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {"color": "blue"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_attribute(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (ATTR, "foo", "1")
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"foo": "1"})


    def test_graph_with_multiple_items(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (ATTR, "foo", "1"),
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "green"}),
            (NODE, "b", {}),
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "blue"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {"color": "green"}), Node("b", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {"color": "blue"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"foo": "1"})


    def test_malformed_graph_data_not_list(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as cm:
            Graph("not a list")
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Graph data malformed")


    def test_malformed_graph_item_not_tuple(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as cm:
            Graph(["not a tuple"])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_malformed_graph_item_empty_tuple(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as cm:
            Graph([()])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_malformed_attribute_missing_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as cm:
            Graph([(ATTR, "no_value")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_malformed_attribute_too_many_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(ATTR, "key", "value", "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Attribute is malformed")


    def test_malformed_attribute_wrong_key_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(ATTR, 123, "value")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Attribute is malformed")


    def test_malformed_attribute_wrong_value_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(ATTR, "key", 123)])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Attribute is malformed")


    def test_malformed_node_missing_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as cm:
            Graph([(NODE, "no_attrs")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_malformed_node_too_many_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(NODE, "name", {}, "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Node is malformed")


    def test_malformed_node_wrong_name_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(NODE, 123, {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Node is malformed")


    def test_malformed_node_wrong_attrs_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(NODE, "name", "not_a_dict")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Node is malformed")


    def test_malformed_edge_missing_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as cm:
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", "dst")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_malformed_edge_too_many_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", "dst", {}, "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Edge is malformed")


    def test_malformed_edge_wrong_src_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(EDGE, 123, "dst", {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Edge is malformed")


    def test_malformed_edge_wrong_dst_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", 123, {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Edge is malformed")


    def test_malformed_edge_wrong_attrs_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", "dst", "not_a_dict")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Edge is malformed")


    def test_unknown_item_type(self):

            Graph([("UNKNOWN", "value")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Unknown item")


    def test_unknown_item_type_with_multiple_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:

        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Unknown item")

class GeneratedTests(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_empty_graph(self):
        graph = Graph()
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])

        ])

    def test_malformed_edge_wrong_src_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(EDGE, 123, "dst", {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Edge is malformed")


    def test_malformed_edge_wrong_dst_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", 123, {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Edge is malformed")


    def test_malformed_edge_wrong_attrs_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", "dst", "not_a_dict")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Edge is malformed")


    def test_unknown_item_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([("UNKNOWN", "value")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Unknown item")


    def test_unknown_item_type_with_multiple_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([("UNKNOWN", "a", "b", {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Unknown item")


    def test_graph_data_malformed_type_none(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as cm:
            Graph(None)
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Graph data malformed")


    def test_graph_item_incomplete_single_element_tuple(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as cm:
            Graph([(NODE,)])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_attribute_is_malformed_none_key(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(ATTR, None, "value")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Attribute is malformed")


    def test_node_is_malformed_none_name(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(NODE, None, {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Node is malformed")


    def test_edge_is_malformed_none_src(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(EDGE, None, "dst", {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Edge is malformed")


    def test_unknown_item_empty_string_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([("", "value")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Unknown item")
```

[ARTIFACT_CONTENT_BEGIN] llm_response_58ddd8f2-a719-47de-92a8-71bc05007674_0_Qwen_Qwen3-Coder-480B-A35B-Instruct-FP8.txt
```python
# main.py
NODE, EDGE, ATTR = range(3)


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name: str, attrs: dict):
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src: str, dst: str, attrs: dict):
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return (self.src == other.src and
                self.dst == other.dst and
                self.attrs == other.attrs)


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data: list | None = None):
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}
        
        if data is None:
            return
            
        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")
            
        for item in data:
            if not isinstance(item, tuple):
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            if len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            item_type = item[0]
            
            if item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) < 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 3:
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                key, value = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value
                
            elif item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) < 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 3:
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                name, attrs = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(name, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))
                
            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) < 4:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 4:
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                src, dst, attrs = item[1], item[2], item[3]
                if not isinstance(src, str) or not isinstance(dst, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))
                
            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")

```

```python
# main.py
NODE, EDGE, ATTR = range(3)


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name: str, attrs: dict):
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src: str, dst: str, attrs: dict):
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return (self.src == other.src and
                self.dst == other.dst and
                self.attrs == other.attrs)


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data: list | None = None):
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}
        
        if data is None:
            return
            
        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")
            
        for item in data:
            if not isinstance(item, tuple):
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            if len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            item_type = item[0]
            
            if item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) < 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 3:
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                key, value = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value
                
            elif item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) < 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 3:
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                name, attrs = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(name, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))
                
            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) < 4:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 4:
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                src, dst, attrs = item[1], item[2], item[3]
                if not isinstance(src, str) or not isinstance(dst, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))
                
            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")
```

```python
# main.py
NODE, EDGE, ATTR = range(3)


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name: str, attrs: dict):
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src: str, dst: str, attrs: dict):
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return (self.src == other.src and
                self.dst == other.dst and
                self.attrs == other.attrs)


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data: list | None = None):
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}
        
        if data is None:
            return
            
        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")
            
        for item in data:
            if not isinstance(item, tuple):
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            if len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            item_type = item[0]
            
            if item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) < 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 3:
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                key, value = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value
                
            elif item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) < 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 3:
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                name, attrs = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(name, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))
                
            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) < 4:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 4:
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                src, dst, attrs = item[1], item[2], item[3]
                if not isinstance(src, str) or not isinstance(dst, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))
                
            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")
```

```python
# main.py
NODE, EDGE, ATTR = range(3)


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name: str, attrs: dict):
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src: str, dst: str, attrs: dict):
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return (self.src == other.src and
                self.dst == other.dst and
                self.attrs == other.attrs)


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data: list | None = None):
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}
        
        if data is None:
            return
            
        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")
            
        for item in data:
            if not isinstance(item, tuple):
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            if len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            item_type = item[0]
            
            if item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed") if len(item) > 3 else TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                key, value = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value
                
            elif item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed") if len(item) > 3 else TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                name, attrs = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(name, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))
                
            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) != 4:
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed") if len(item) > 4 else TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                src, dst, attrs = item[1], item[2], item[3]
                if not isinstance(src, str) or not isinstance(dst, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))
                
            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")
```

[ARTIFACT_CONTENT_BEGIN] llm_response_bdb994b5-cb70-48f1-ac19-6bd1bb400e8c_0_Qwen_Qwen3-Coder-480B-A35B-Instruct-FP8.txt
```python
# main.py
NODE, EDGE, ATTR = range(3)


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name: str, attrs: dict):
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src: str, dst: str, attrs: dict):
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return (self.src == other.src and
                self.dst == other.dst and
                self.attrs == other.attrs)


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data: list | None = None):
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}
        
        if data is None:
            return
            
        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")
            
        for item in data:
            if not isinstance(item, tuple):
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            if len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            item_type = item[0]
            
            if item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) < 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 3:
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                key, value = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value
                
            elif item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) < 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 3:
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                name, attrs = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(name, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))
                
            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) < 4:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 4:
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                src, dst, attrs = item[1], item[2], item[3]
                if not isinstance(src, str) or not isinstance(dst, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))
                
            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")
```

```python
import unittest
from main import Graph, Node, Edge, NODE, EDGE, ATTR


class DotDslTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_empty_graph(self):
        graph = Graph()
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_with_one_node(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (NODE, "a", {})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_with_one_node_with_attrs(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "green"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {"color": "green"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_with_one_edge(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_with_one_edge_with_attrs(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "blue"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {"color": "blue"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_with_one_attribute(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (ATTR, "foo", "1")
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"foo": "1"})

    def test_graph_with_attributes_nodes_and_edges(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (ATTR, "foo", "1"),
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "green"}),
            (NODE, "c", {}),
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "blue"}),
            (EDGE, "c", "d", {})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"foo": "1"})
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {"color": "green"}), Node("c", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {"color": "blue"}), Edge("c", "d", {})])

    def test_malformed_graph_data_not_a_list(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as err:
            Graph("not a list")
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Graph data malformed")

    def test_malformed_graph_item_not_a_tuple(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as err:
            Graph(["not a tuple"])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_malformed_graph_item_empty_tuple(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as err:
            Graph([()])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_malformed_attribute_missing_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as err:
            Graph([(ATTR, "no_value")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_malformed_attribute_too_many_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(ATTR, "key", "value", "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Attribute is malformed")

    def test_malformed_attribute_non_string_key(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(ATTR, 123, "value")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Attribute is malformed")

    def test_malformed_attribute_non_string_value(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(ATTR, "key", 123)])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Attribute is malformed")

    def test_malformed_node_missing_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as err:
            Graph([(NODE, "no_attrs")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_malformed_node_too_many_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(NODE, "name", {}, "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Node is malformed")

    def test_malformed_node_non_string_name(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(NODE, 123, {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Node is malformed")

    def test_malformed_node_non_dict_attrs(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(NODE, "name", "not a dict")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Node is malformed")

    def test_malformed_edge_missing_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as err:
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", "dst")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_malformed_edge_too_many_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", "dst", {}, "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Edge is malformed")

    def test_malformed_edge_non_string_src(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(EDGE, 123, "dst", {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Edge is malformed")

    def test_malformed_edge_non_string_dst(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", 123, {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Edge is malformed")

    def test_malformed_edge_non_dict_attrs(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", "dst", "not a dict")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Edge is malformed")

    def test_unknown_item_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(99, "unknown")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Unknown item")

    unittest.main()

```python
import unittest
from main import Graph, Node, Edge, NODE, EDGE, ATTR


class DotDSLTest(unittest.TestCase):

    # 1) successful construction/usage
    def test_empty_graph(self):
        g = Graph()
        self.assertEqual(g.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(g.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(g.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_with_attrs(self):
        g = Graph([(ATTR, "bgcolor", "yellow")])
        self.assertEqual(g.attrs, {"bgcolor": "yellow"})

    def test_graph_with_node(self):
        g = Graph([(NODE, "a", {"color": "red"})])
        self.assertEqual(len(g.nodes), 1)
        self.assertEqual(g.nodes[0], Node("a", {"color": "red"}))

    def test_graph_with_edge(self):
        g = Graph([(EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "green"})])
        self.assertEqual(len(g.edges), 1)
        self.assertEqual(g.edges[0], Edge("a", "b", {"color": "green"}))

    def test_full_graph(self):
        data = [
            (ATTR, "bgcolor", "yellow"),
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "red"}),
            (NODE, "b", {"color": "blue"}),
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "green"}),
        ]
        g = Graph(data)
        self.assertEqual(g.attrs, {"bgcolor": "yellow"})
        self.assertEqual(len(g.nodes), 2)
        self.assertEqual(len(g.edges), 1)
        self.assertEqual(g.edges[0], Edge("a", "b", {"color": "green"}))

    # 2) invalid input type
    def test_graph_data_not_list(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph data malformed"):
            Graph("not a list")

    # 3) shape/arity errors
    def test_graph_item_empty_tuple(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph item incomplete"):
            Graph([()])

    def test_attr_item_incomplete(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph item incomplete"):
            Graph([(ATTR, "key")])

    def test_node_item_incomplete(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph item incomplete"):
            Graph([(NODE, "name")])

    def test_edge_item_incomplete(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph item incomplete"):
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", "dst")])

    # 4) value/type validation errors
    def test_attr_malformed_extra_element(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Attribute is malformed"):
            Graph([(ATTR, "key", "value", "extra")])

    def test_attr_malformed_non_str_key(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Attribute is malformed"):
            Graph([(ATTR, 123, "value")])

    def test_attr_malformed_non_str_value(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Attribute is malformed"):
            Graph([(ATTR, "key", 123)])

    def test_node_malformed_extra_element(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Node is malformed"):
            Graph([(NODE, "name", {}, "extra")])

    def test_node_malformed_non_str_name(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Node is malformed"):
            Graph([(NODE, 123, {})])

    def test_node_malformed_non_dict_attrs(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Node is malformed"):
            Graph([(NODE, "name", "not a dict")])

    def test_edge_malformed_extra_element(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Edge is malformed"):
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", "dst", {}, "extra")])

    def test_edge_malformed_non_str_src(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Edge is malformed"):
            Graph([(EDGE, 123, "dst", {})])

    def test_edge_malformed_non_str_dst(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Edge is malformed"):
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", 123, {})])

    def test_edge_malformed_non_dict_attrs(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Edge is malformed"):
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", "dst", "not a dict")])

    # 5) unknown/unsupported case
    def test_unknown_item_type(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Unknown item"):
            Graph([(99, "unknown")])

    def test_graph_item_not_tuple(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph item incomplete"):
            Graph(["not a tuple"])

```python
import unittest
from main import Graph, Node, Edge, NODE, EDGE, ATTR


class DotDslTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_empty_graph(self):
        graph = Graph()
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_with_attributes(self):
        graph = Graph([(ATTR, "bgcolor", "yellow")])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"bgcolor": "yellow"})
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])

    def test_graph_with_node(self):
        graph = Graph([(NODE, "a", {"color": "red"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {"color": "red"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_with_edge(self):
        graph = Graph([(EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "green"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {"color": "green"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_with_multiple_items(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (ATTR, "bgcolor", "yellow"),
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "red"}),
            (NODE, "b", {"color": "blue"}),
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "green"}),
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"bgcolor": "yellow"})
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [
            Node("a", {"color": "red"}),
            Node("b", {"color": "blue"}),
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {"color": "green"})])

    def test_malformed_graph_data(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph("not a list")
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph data malformed")

    def test_incomplete_graph_item(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([()])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_incomplete_attribute(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([(ATTR, "key")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_malformed_attribute(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(ATTR, "key", "value", "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Attribute is malformed")
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(ATTR, 123, "value")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Attribute is malformed")

    def test_incomplete_node(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([(NODE, "name")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_malformed_node(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(NODE, "name", {}, "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Node is malformed")
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(NODE, 123, {"color": "red"})])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Node is malformed")

    def test_incomplete_edge(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", "dst")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_malformed_edge(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", "dst", {}, "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Edge is malformed")
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, 123, "dst", {"color": "green"})])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Edge is malformed")

    def test_unknown_item_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(999, "unknown", "item")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Unknown item")

    def test_node_equality(self):
        node1 = Node("a", {"color": "red"})
        node2 = Node("a", {"color": "red"})
        self.assertEqual(node1, node2)

    def test_edge_equality(self):
        edge1 = Edge("a", "b", {"color": "green"})
        edge2 = Edge("a", "b", {"color": "green"})
        self.assertEqual(edge1, edge2)

    def test_mixed_valid_and_invalid_items(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([
                (ATTR, "bgcolor", "yellow"),
                (NODE, "a", {"color": "red"}),
                (999, "unknown", "item"),
            ])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Unknown item")
```
