import unittest
from main import Graph, Node, Edge, NODE, EDGE, ATTR

class DotDslTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_empty_graph(self):
        graph = Graph()
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_with_attributes(self):
        data = [(ATTR, "bgcolor", "yellow")]
        graph = Graph(data)
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"bgcolor": "yellow"})

    def test_graph_with_nodes(self):
        data = [(NODE, "a", {"color": "red"}), (NODE, "b", {"color": "blue"})]
        graph = Graph(data)
        self.assertEqual(len(graph.nodes), 2)
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes[0].name, "a")
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes[0].attrs, {"color": "red"})
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes[1].name, "b")
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes[1].attrs, {"color": "blue"})

    def test_graph_with_edges(self):
        data = [(EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "green"})]
        graph = Graph(data)
        self.assertEqual(len(graph.edges), 1)
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges[0].src, "a")
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges[0].dst, "b")
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges[0].attrs, {"color": "green"})

    def test_graph_with_mixed_items(self):
        data = [
            (ATTR, "bgcolor", "yellow"),
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "red"}),
            (NODE, "b", {"color": "blue"}),
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "green"})
        ]
        graph = Graph(data)
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"bgcolor": "yellow"})
        self.assertEqual(len(graph.nodes), 2)
        self.assertEqual(len(graph.edges), 1)

    def test_invalid_graph_data_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as cm:
            Graph("not a list")
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Graph data malformed")

    def test_empty_tuple_in_data(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as cm:
            Graph([()])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_incomplete_attr_item(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as cm:
            Graph([(ATTR, "key")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_incomplete_node_item(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as cm:
            Graph([(NODE, "name")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_incomplete_edge_item(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as cm:
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", "dst")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_attr_with_extra_elements(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(ATTR, "key", "value", "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Attribute is malformed")

    def test_node_with_extra_elements(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(NODE, "name", {}, "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Node is malformed")

    def test_edge_with_extra_elements(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", "dst", {}, "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Edge is malformed")

    def test_attr_with_non_string_key(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(ATTR, 123, "value")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Attribute is malformed")

    def test_attr_with_non_string_value(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(ATTR, "key", 123)])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Attribute is malformed")

    def test_node_with_non_string_name(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(NODE, 123, {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Node is malformed")

    def test_node_with_non_dict_attrs(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(NODE, "name", "not a dict")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Node is malformed")

    def test_edge_with_non_string_src(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(EDGE, 123, "dst", {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Edge is malformed")

    def test_edge_with_non_string_dst(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", 123, {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Edge is malformed")

    def test_edge_with_non_dict_attrs(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", "dst", "not a dict")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Edge is malformed")

    def test_unknown_item_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            Graph([(99, "unknown", "item")])
        self.assertEqual(str(cm.exception), "Unknown item")

This takes a `list` of one \nor more tuples that describe:\n\n+ attributes\n+ `Nodes`\n+ `Edges`\n\nThe implementations of a `Node` and an `Edge` are provided in `dot_dsl.py`.\n\nFor more details on the DSL's expected design and the expected error types and messages, take a look at the test cases in `dot_dsl_test.py` \n\n\n## Exception messages\n\nSometimes it is necessary to [raise an exception](https://docs.python.org/3/tutorial/errors.html#raising-exceptions). When you do this, you should always include a **meaningful error message** to indicate what the source of the error is. This makes your code more readable and helps significantly with debugging. For situations where you know that the error source will be a certain type, you can choose to raise one of the [built in error types](https://docs.python.org/3/library/exceptions.html#base-classes), but should still include a meaningful message.\n\nThis particular exercise requires that you use the [raise statement](https://docs.python.org/3/reference/simple_stmts.html#the-raise-statement) to \"throw\" a `TypeError` for when a `Graph` is malformed, and a `ValueError` when an `Edge`, `Node`, or `attribute` is malformed. The tests will only pass if you both `raise` the `exception` and include a message with it.\n\nTo raise an error with a message, write the message as an argument to the `exception` type:\n\n```python\n# Graph is malformed\nraise TypeError(\"Graph data malformed\")\n\n# Graph item is incomplete (empty or incomplete tuple)\nraise TypeError(\"Graph item incomplete\")\n\n# Attribute has incorrect values\nraise ValueError(\"Attribute is malformed\")\n\n# Node has incorrect values\nraise ValueError(\"Node is malformed\")\n\n# Edge has incorrect values\nraise ValueError(\"Edge is malformed\")\n\n# Unknown item type\nraise ValueError(\"Unknown item\")\n```\n\n\nTask: Given a list of unittest methods, score each one's correctness vs the spec (0-100).\nReturn ONLY JSON mapping id->score, e.g., {\"m1\": 80, \"m2\": 55}."

```python
import unittest
from main import *

class GeneratedTests(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_empty_graph(self):
        graph = Graph()
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_node(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (NODE, "a", {})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_node_with_attrs(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "green"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {"color": "green"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_edge(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_edge_with_attrs(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "blue"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {"color": "blue"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_attribute(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (ATTR, "foo", "1")
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"foo": "1"})


    def test_graph_with_attributes_nodes_and_edges(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (ATTR, "foo", "1"),
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "green"}),
            (NODE, "c", {}),
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "blue"}),
            (EDGE, "c", "d", {})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"foo": "1"})
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {"color": "green"}), Node("c", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {"color": "blue"}), Edge("c", "d", {})])


    def test_malformed_graph_data_not_a_list(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as err:
            Graph("not a list")
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Graph data malformed")


    def test_malformed_graph_item_not_a_tuple(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as err:
            Graph(["not a tuple"])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_malformed_graph_item_empty_tuple(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as err:
            Graph([()])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_malformed_attribute_missing_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as err:
            Graph([(ATTR, "no_value")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_malformed_attribute_too_many_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(ATTR, "key", "value", "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Attribute is malformed")


    def test_malformed_attribute_non_string_key(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(ATTR, 123, "value")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Attribute is malformed")


    def test_malformed_attribute_non_string_value(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(ATTR, "key", 123)])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Attribute is malformed")


    def test_malformed_node_missing_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as err:
            Graph([(NODE, "no_attrs")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_malformed_node_too_many_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(NODE, "name", {}, "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Node is malformed")


    def test_malformed_node_non_string_name(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(NODE, 123, {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Node is malformed")


    def test_malformed_node_non_dict_attrs(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(NODE, "name", "not a dict")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Node is malformed")


    def test_malformed_edge_missing_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as err:
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", "dst")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_malformed_edge_too_many_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", "dst", {}, "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Edge is malformed")


    def test_malformed_edge_non_string_src(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(EDGE, 123, "dst", {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Edge is malformed")


    def test_malformed_edge_non_string_dst(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", 123, {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Edge is malformed")


    def test_malformed_edge_non_dict_attrs(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", "dst", "not a dict")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Edge is malformed")


    def test_unknown_item_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([("UNKNOWN", "a", "b")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Unknown item")


    def test_unknown_item_type_with_no_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([("UNKNOWN",)])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Unknown item")


    def test_unknown_item_type_with_many_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([("UNKNOWN", "a", "b", "c", "d")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Unknown item")

```

```python
import unittest
from main import *

class GeneratedTests(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_empty_graph(self):
        graph = Graph()
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_node(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (NODE, "a", {})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_node_with_attrs(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "green"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {"color": "green"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_edge(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_edge_with_attrs(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "blue"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {"color": "blue"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_attribute(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (ATTR, "foo", "1")
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"foo": "1"})


    def test_graph_with_attributes_nodes_and_edges(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (ATTR, "foo", "1"),
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "green"}),
            (NODE, "c", {}),
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "blue"}),
            (EDGE, "c", "d", {})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"foo": "1"})
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {"color": "green"}), Node("c", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {"color": "blue"}), Edge("c", "d", {})])


    def test_malformed_graph_data_not_a_list(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as err:
            Graph("not a list")
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Graph data malformed")


    def test_malformed_graph_item_not_a_tuple(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as err:
            Graph(["not a tuple"])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_malformed_graph_item_empty_tuple(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as err:
            Graph([()])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_malformed_attribute_missing_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as err:
            Graph([(ATTR, "no_value")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_malformed_attribute_too_many_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(ATTR, "key", "value", "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Attribute is malformed")


    def test_malformed_attribute_non_string_key(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(ATTR, 123, "value")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Attribute is malformed")


    def test_malformed_attribute_non_string_value(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(ATTR, "key", 123)])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Attribute is malformed")


    def test_malformed_node_missing_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as err:
            Graph([(NODE, "no_attrs")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_malformed_node_too_many_values(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(NODE, "name", {}, "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Node is malformed")

        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as err:
            Graph([(EDGE, "src", "dst", {}, "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Edge is malformed")


    def test_malformed_edge_non_string_src(self):

        self.assertEqual(str(err.exception), "Edge is malformed")

            Graph([(EDGE, "src", "dst", "not a dict")])

[ARTIFACT_CONTENT_BEGIN] llm_response_c57844a0-9c5f-4a9a-bb0c-8db086a54d4a_0_Qwen_Qwen3-Coder-480B-A35B-Instruct-FP8.txt
```python
# main.py
NODE, EDGE, ATTR = range(3)


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name: str, attrs: dict):
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src: str, dst: str, attrs: dict):
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return (self.src == other.src and
                self.dst == other.dst and
                self.attrs == other.attrs)


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data: list | None = None):
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}
        
        if data is None:
            return
            
        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")
            
        for item in data:
            if not isinstance(item, tuple):
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            if len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            item_type = item[0]
            
            if item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) < 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 3:
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                key, value = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value
                
            elif item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) < 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 3:
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                name, attrs = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(name, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))
                
            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) < 4:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 4:
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                src, dst, attrs = item[1], item[2], item[3]
                if not isinstance(src, str) or not isinstance(dst, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))
                
            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")

```

```python
# main.py
NODE, EDGE, ATTR = range(3)


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name: str, attrs: dict):
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src: str, dst: str, attrs: dict):
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return (self.src == other.src and
                self.dst == other.dst and
                self.attrs == other.attrs)


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data: list | None = None):
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}
        
        if data is None:
            return
            
        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")
            
        for item in data:
            if not isinstance(item, tuple):
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            if len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            item_type = item[0]
            
            if item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) < 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 3:
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                key, value = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value
                
            elif item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) < 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 3:
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                name, attrs = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(name, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))
                
            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) < 4:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 4:
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                src, dst, attrs = item[1], item[2], item[3]
                if not isinstance(src, str) or not isinstance(dst, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))
                
            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")
```

```python
# main.py
NODE, EDGE, ATTR = range(3)


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name: str, attrs: dict):
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src: str, dst: str, attrs: dict):
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return (self.src == other.src and
                self.dst == other.dst and
                self.attrs == other.attrs)


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data: list | None = None):
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}
        
        if data is None:
            return
            
        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")
            
        for item in data:
            if not isinstance(item, tuple):
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            if len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            item_type = item[0]
            
            if item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) < 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 3:
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                key, value = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value
                
            elif item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) < 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 3:
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                name, attrs = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(name, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))
                
            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) < 4:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 4:
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                src, dst, attrs = item[1], item[2], item[3]
                if not isinstance(src, str) or not isinstance(dst, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))
                
            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")
```

# main.py
NODE, EDGE, ATTR = range(3)


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name: str, attrs: dict):
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src: str, dst: str, attrs: dict):
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return (self.src == other.src and
                self.dst == other.dst and
                self.attrs == other.attrs)


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data: list | None = None):
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}
        
        if data is None:
            return
            
        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")
            
        for item in data:
            if not isinstance(item, tuple):
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            if len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            item_type = item[0]
            
            if item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed") if len(item) > 3 else TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                key, value = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value
                
            elif item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed") if len(item) > 3 else TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                name, attrs = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(name, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))
                
            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) != 4:
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed") if len(item) > 4 else TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                src, dst, attrs = item[1], item[2], item[3]
                if not isinstance(src, str) or not isinstance(dst, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))
                
            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")
```

```python
NODE, EDGE, ATTR = range(3)


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name: str, attrs: dict):
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src: str, dst: str, attrs: dict):
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return (self.src == other.src and
                self.dst == other.dst and
                self.attrs == other.attrs)


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data: list | None = None):
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}
        
        if data is None:
            return
            
        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")
            
        for item in data:
            if not isinstance(item, tuple):
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            if len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            item_type = item[0]
            
            if item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) < 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 3:
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                key, value = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value
                
            elif item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) < 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 3:
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                name, attrs = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(name, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))
                
            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) < 4:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                if len(item) > 4:
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                src, dst, attrs = item[1], item[2], item[3]
                if not isinstance(src, str) or not isinstance(dst, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))
                
            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")
```
